```text
This is a very significant result! The D111 direction check sweep revealed massive corrections across all 9 α values. Key findings:

1. **diff_fwd drops to 0 in 7/9 α values** — diff_xcorr almost always has negative peak lag
2. **naive_fwd drops to 0 at α≥0.75** — at high contamination, cross-correlation peaks in reverse direction
3. **K≥4 collapses to 0 at α=0.75 and α=1.00** — but K≥3 holds at 0.750 because threshold+PermG+TY are unaffected


4. **At high α, only {threshold, PermG, TY} sustain forward evidence** — this strongly supports the D106 decision to prioritize PermG, since the xcorr methods have fundamental directional biases

Now I need to verify the audit report and CSV updates are correct, check that the registry reflects these changes, report the findings to the Director, and update the ROADMAP accordingly.
```

_source line 2003 · 2026-05-19T23:52:34.127Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Both CSVs are updated. Let me verify the key patterns:

- **diff_rate**: 0 in 8/12 α values (only 0.10=0.125, 0.25=0.250, 0.30=0.375, 0.45=0.375 have any forward diff)
- **naive_rate at α≥0.75**: 0.000 — confirmed


- **K≥4 at α=0.75, 1.00**: 0.000 — confirmed
- **K≥3 at α=0.75, 1.00**: 0.750 — held by threshold+PermG+TY
- **PermG unchanged**: all values same as before (0.000 for null, 0.750 at onset, etc.)

The direction check for D111 dramatically weakens the xcorr evidence while leaving PermG completely unaffected, and K≥4 essentially vanishes at higher α values, making K≥3 the only meaningful threshold.

The onset signal at α=0.50 drops from K≥4=0.750 to 0.250, though K≥3 remains solid at 0.625, and the sync_collapse zone at α=0.60 shows an even more dramatic K≥4 collapse from 0.875 to 0.125—the TY=100% is what preserves K≥3=0.750 there. Interestingly, α=0.30 now shows K≥4=0.375, the highest K≥4 in the null zone, though that's likely just noise from the single seed.

Looking at the null control FPR at α=0.00, the differential direction check is doing excellent work—diff_fwd drops from 0.500 to 0.000 while naive_fwd improves from 1.000 to 0.625. The K≥3 metric improves substantially from 0.375 to 0.125, and K≥4 stays perfect at 0.000. Overall, the FPR numbers look promising for the paper: PermG and K≥4 are both at 0%, K≥3 improved to 12.5% from 37.5%, and even naive FPR dropped to 62.5% from 100%.

Now I need to verify the audit report, confirm the registry notes are in place, then report these results to the Director and update the roadmap.
```

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research experiment management session for the `distrib_canary_collapse` project targeting EMNLP 2026 (DDL ~June 15). The agent acts as scheduler/coordinator, spawning Workers for SSH execution, analysis, and experiment submission. Key activities in this session:
   - Session initialization (registry sync, cron reconciliation, state verification)
   - Registering missing cron for a050_postd093 experiment (PID 205750)
   - Processing pythia410m_wiki_a100_s43_fp32 completion (PermG=6/8, cross-seed validated)
   - D111 direction check full sweep audit of 9 WikiText α values (0.00-1.00)
   - Emergency disk cleanup on westd-19828 (100%→64%, saving opt13b_s43)
   - SmolLM3 s43 diagnosis (disk full failure) and retry on 17855:cuda:3
   - PREPARING queue cleanup (4 experiments killed as superseded)
   - α=0.00 naive_fwd correction (Worker arithmetic error: 5/8→6/8)
   - Evaluating 19828:cuda:1 allocation candidates from experiment_plan

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **MMCT (Multi-Method Consensus Testing)**: 5-method framework with PermG fwd as primary indicator
   - **D105 standard**: True 3σ threshold, BH FDR per-method, unified 8-pair (canary={token_entropy, ece} × downstream={d1, d2, d3, mauve}), n_perm=5000
   - **D111 direction check**: naive_xcorr and diff_xcorr must verify peak_lag > 0 for forward direction
   - **D115 K≥4 deprecation**: After D111 full sweep, K≥4 collapsed to 0 in 7/9 α values; demoted to appendix-only. K≥3 becomes the sole consensus metric, driven by {threshold, PermG, TY}
   - **D093 padding confound**: Onset boundary [0.45, 0.50] coincides with padding code change; a050_postd093 experiment validates
   - **4-Regime Dose-Response**: null (α≤0.45, PermG=0%), onset ([0.45,0.50]), sync_collapse (α=0.60), detection (α≥0.75)
   - **D079**: fp32 mandatory for models ≤3B
   - **Registry as truth source**: read_registry tool returns stale/cached data — always verify with grep on registry.yaml
   - **State machine**: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE (can't skip states; PREPARING→DONE requires using conclusion=killed)
